### 词群 09.01｜演出怎样被制作和传播 {#group-09-01}

**串联方式：** 从表演、排练进入记者、编辑、评论员等角色，再制作宣传材料并出版传播。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-001">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-001" class="vocabulary-word-title">perform<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-001" aria-label="定位 perform">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-001" data-mark-label="perform" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="perform：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="perform：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perform&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 perform" title="在有道查询 perform">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-001</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 履行，执行</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The actors used film as a medium to review their rehearsal before they would <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perform&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>perform</strong></a> on stage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">演员用电影影像这一媒介回看排练，然后才登台演出。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At rehearsal, students explored how to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perform&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>perform</strong></a> the story through the medium of dance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">排练时，学生探索如何通过舞蹈媒介表演这个故事。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A recorded rehearsal can reveal how differently actors <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perform&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>perform</strong></a> for the medium of television.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">录下的排练能显示演员为电视媒介表演时有何不同。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">perform on stage</span> · 登台表演</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=performance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">performance</a></span> · 演出</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=audience&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">audience</a></span> · 观众</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-002">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-002" class="vocabulary-word-title">rehearsal<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-002" aria-label="定位 rehearsal">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-002" data-mark-label="rehearsal" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="rehearsal：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="rehearsal：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rehearsal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 rehearsal" title="在有道查询 rehearsal">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-002</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 排练</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The actors used film as a medium to review their <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rehearsal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rehearsal</strong></a> before they would perform on stage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">演员用电影影像这一媒介回看排练，然后才登台演出。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rehearsal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rehearsal</strong></a>, students explored how to perform the story through the medium of dance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">排练时，学生探索如何通过舞蹈媒介表演这个故事。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A recorded <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rehearsal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rehearsal</strong></a> can reveal how differently actors perform for the medium of television.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">录下的排练能显示演员为电视媒介表演时有何不同。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a dress rehearsal</span> · 彩排</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=performance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">performance</a></span> · 演出</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=audience&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">audience</a></span> · 观众</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At rehearsal, students explored how to perform the story through the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=medium&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>medium</strong></a> of dance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">排练时，学生探索如何通过舞蹈媒介表演这个故事。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-004">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-004" class="vocabulary-word-title">journalist<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-004" aria-label="定位 journalist">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-004" data-mark-label="journalist" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="journalist：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="journalist：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=journalist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 journalist" title="在有道查询 journalist">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-004</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 记者，新闻工作者</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=journalist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>journalist</strong></a> interviewed a theatre critic and a television commentator for the press report.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">记者采访了一位剧评人和一位电视评论员，用于新闻报道。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A critic reviewed the play, a commentator discussed its politics, and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=journalist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>journalist</strong></a> summarised both views for the press.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">评论家评论了戏剧，评论员讨论其政治含义，记者则为报刊概述了两种观点。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The press conference allowed a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=journalist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>journalist</strong></a>, a critic and a commentator to question the director.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">新闻发布会让记者、评论家和评论员有机会向导演提问。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an investigative journalist</span> · 调查记者</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=review&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">review</a></span> · 评论</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=broadcast&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">broadcast</a></span> · 广播节目</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-005">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-005" class="vocabulary-word-title">press<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-005" aria-label="定位 press">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-005" data-mark-label="press" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="press：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="press：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=press&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 press" title="在有道查询 press">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-005</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 新闻界，记者；报刊；出版社</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The journalist interviewed a theatre critic and a television commentator for the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=press&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>press</strong></a> report.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">记者采访了一位剧评人和一位电视评论员，用于新闻报道。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A critic reviewed the play, a commentator discussed its politics, and a journalist summarised both views for the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=press&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>press</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">评论家评论了戏剧，评论员讨论其政治含义，记者则为报刊概述了两种观点。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=press&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>press</strong></a> conference allowed a journalist, a critic and a commentator to question the director.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">新闻发布会让记者、评论家和评论员有机会向导演提问。</p></li>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The journalist interviewed a theatre critic and a television <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commentator&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>commentator</strong></a> for the press report.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">记者采访了一位剧评人和一位电视评论员，用于新闻报道。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A critic reviewed the play, a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commentator&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>commentator</strong></a> discussed its politics, and a journalist summarised both views for the press.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">评论家评论了戏剧，评论员讨论其政治含义，记者则为报刊概述了两种观点。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The press conference allowed a journalist, a critic and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commentator&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>commentator</strong></a> to question the director.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">新闻发布会让记者、评论家和评论员有机会向导演提问。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a sports commentator</span> · 体育解说员</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=review&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">review</a></span> · 评论</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=broadcast&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">broadcast</a></span> · 广播节目</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-008">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-008" class="vocabulary-word-title">exponent<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-008" aria-label="定位 exponent">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-008" data-mark-label="exponent" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="exponent：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="exponent：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exponent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 exponent" title="在有道查询 exponent">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-008</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 阐述者，倡导者，拥护者</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The announcer introduced a correspondent interviewing a leading <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exponent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>exponent</strong></a> of jazz when a messenger delivered the revised schedule.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">播音员介绍了一位正在采访重要爵士乐倡导者的记者，此时信使送来了修订后的时间表。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A messenger brought the statement from the arts <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exponent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>exponent</strong></a> to the correspondent, who passed it to the announcer.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">信使将这位艺术倡导者的声明送给记者，记者又交给了播音员。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The correspondent spoke to an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exponent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>exponent</strong></a> of traditional music while the announcer waited for a messenger carrying the recording.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">记者采访传统音乐倡导者时，播音员正等着送录音的信使。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an exponent of jazz</span> · 爵士乐倡导者</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dispatch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dispatch</a></span> · 通讯报道</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=advocate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">advocate</a></span> · 倡导者</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-009">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-009" class="vocabulary-word-title">announcer<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-009" aria-label="定位 announcer">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-009" data-mark-label="announcer" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="announcer：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="announcer：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=announcer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 announcer" title="在有道查询 announcer">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-009</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 广播员，播音员</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=announcer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>announcer</strong></a> introduced a correspondent interviewing a leading exponent of jazz when a messenger delivered the revised schedule.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">播音员介绍了一位正在采访重要爵士乐倡导者的记者，此时信使送来了修订后的时间表。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A messenger brought the statement from the arts exponent to the correspondent, who passed it to the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=announcer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>announcer</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">信使将这位艺术倡导者的声明送给记者，记者又交给了播音员。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The correspondent spoke to an exponent of traditional music while the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=announcer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>announcer</strong></a> waited for a messenger carrying the recording.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">记者采访传统音乐倡导者时，播音员正等着送录音的信使。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a radio announcer</span> · 电台播音员</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dispatch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dispatch</a></span> · 通讯报道</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=advocate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">advocate</a></span> · 倡导者</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-010">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-010" class="vocabulary-word-title">correspondent<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-010" aria-label="定位 correspondent">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-010" data-mark-label="correspondent" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="correspondent：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="correspondent：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=correspondent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 correspondent" title="在有道查询 correspondent">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-010</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 通信者；通讯员，记者</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The announcer introduced a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=correspondent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>correspondent</strong></a> interviewing a leading exponent of jazz when a messenger delivered the revised schedule.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">播音员介绍了一位正在采访重要爵士乐倡导者的记者，此时信使送来了修订后的时间表。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A messenger brought the statement from the arts exponent to the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=correspondent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>correspondent</strong></a>, who passed it to the announcer.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">信使将这位艺术倡导者的声明送给记者，记者又交给了播音员。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=correspondent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>correspondent</strong></a> spoke to an exponent of traditional music while the announcer waited for a messenger carrying the recording.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">记者采访传统音乐倡导者时，播音员正等着送录音的信使。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a foreign correspondent</span> · 驻外记者</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dispatch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dispatch</a></span> · 通讯报道</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=advocate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">advocate</a></span> · 倡导者</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-011">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-011" class="vocabulary-word-title">messenger<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-011" aria-label="定位 messenger">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-011" data-mark-label="messenger" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="messenger：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="messenger：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=messenger&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 messenger" title="在有道查询 messenger">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-011</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 邮递员，信使</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The announcer introduced a correspondent interviewing a leading exponent of jazz when a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=messenger&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>messenger</strong></a> delivered the revised schedule.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">播音员介绍了一位正在采访重要爵士乐倡导者的记者，此时信使送来了修订后的时间表。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=messenger&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>messenger</strong></a> brought the statement from the arts exponent to the correspondent, who passed it to the announcer.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">信使将这位艺术倡导者的声明送给记者，记者又交给了播音员。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The correspondent spoke to an exponent of traditional music while the announcer waited for a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=messenger&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>messenger</strong></a> carrying the recording.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">记者采访传统音乐倡导者时，播音员正等着送录音的信使。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">send a messenger</span> · 派信使</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dispatch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dispatch</a></span> · 通讯报道</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=advocate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">advocate</a></span> · 倡导者</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-012">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-012" class="vocabulary-word-title">editor<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-012" aria-label="定位 editor">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-012" data-mark-label="editor" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="editor：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="editor：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=editor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 editor" title="在有道查询 editor">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-012</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 编辑，校订者；(电台或电视台的)记者，编辑</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=editor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>editor</strong></a> asked the typist to prepare a handout and a leaflet for the launch.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">编辑请打字员为发布活动准备讲义和传单。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After checking the leaflet, the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=editor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>editor</strong></a> returned the handout to the typist for corrections.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">编辑检查传单后，把讲义退给打字员修改。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A typist helped the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=editor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>editor</strong></a> turn the long handout into a short publicity leaflet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位打字员帮助编辑把长篇资料改成简短的宣传传单。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a newspaper editor</span> · 报纸编辑</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=proofreading&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">proofreading</a></span> · 校对</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=publicity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">publicity</a></span> · 宣传</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-013">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-013" class="vocabulary-word-title">typist<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-013" aria-label="定位 typist">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-013" data-mark-label="typist" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="typist：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="typist：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=typist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 typist" title="在有道查询 typist">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-013</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 打字员</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The editor asked the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=typist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>typist</strong></a> to prepare a handout and a leaflet for the launch.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">编辑请打字员为发布活动准备讲义和传单。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After checking the leaflet, the editor returned the handout to the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=typist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>typist</strong></a> for corrections.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">编辑检查传单后，把讲义退给打字员修改。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=typist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>typist</strong></a> helped the editor turn the long handout into a short publicity leaflet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位打字员帮助编辑把长篇资料改成简短的宣传传单。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a skilled typist</span> · 熟练打字员</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=proofreading&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">proofreading</a></span> · 校对</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=publicity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">publicity</a></span> · 宣传</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-014">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-014" class="vocabulary-word-title">handout<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-014" aria-label="定位 handout">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-014" data-mark-label="handout" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="handout：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="handout：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=handout&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 handout" title="在有道查询 handout">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-014</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 传单；讲义</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The editor asked the typist to prepare a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=handout&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>handout</strong></a> and a leaflet for the launch.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">编辑请打字员为发布活动准备讲义和传单。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After checking the leaflet, the editor returned the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=handout&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>handout</strong></a> to the typist for corrections.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">编辑检查传单后，把讲义退给打字员修改。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A typist helped the editor turn the long <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=handout&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>handout</strong></a> into a short publicity leaflet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位打字员帮助编辑把长篇资料改成简短的宣传传单。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a press handout</span> · 新闻资料</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=proofreading&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">proofreading</a></span> · 校对</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=publicity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">publicity</a></span> · 宣传</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After checking the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=leaflet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>leaflet</strong></a>, the editor returned the handout to the typist for corrections.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">编辑检查传单后，把讲义退给打字员修改。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A typist helped the editor turn the long handout into a short publicity <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=leaflet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>leaflet</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位打字员帮助编辑把长篇资料改成简短的宣传传单。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-016">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-016" class="vocabulary-word-title">propaganda<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-016" aria-label="定位 propaganda">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-016" data-mark-label="propaganda" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="propaganda：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="propaganda：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=propaganda&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 propaganda" title="在有道查询 propaganda">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-016</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 宣传，鼓吹</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The platform refused to publish material designed to disseminate <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=propaganda&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>propaganda</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">平台拒绝发布旨在传播政治宣传的材料。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Newspapers can publish corrections, but <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=propaganda&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>propaganda</strong></a> may still disseminate quickly online.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报纸可以刊登更正，但政治宣传仍可能在网上迅速传播。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The researchers will publish a study of how groups disseminate <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=propaganda&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>propaganda</strong></a> through social media.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究者将发表一项关于团体如何通过社交媒体传播政治宣传的研究。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">political propaganda</span> · 政治宣传</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=misinformation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">misinformation</a></span> · 错误信息</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=media%20literacy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">media literacy</a></span> · 媒介素养</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-017">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-017" class="vocabulary-word-title">publish<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-017" aria-label="定位 publish">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-017" data-mark-label="publish" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="publish：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="publish：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=publish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 publish" title="在有道查询 publish">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-017</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 公布；出版，发行</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The platform refused to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=publish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>publish</strong></a> material designed to disseminate propaganda.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">平台拒绝发布旨在传播政治宣传的材料。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Newspapers can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=publish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>publish</strong></a> corrections, but propaganda may still disseminate quickly online.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报纸可以刊登更正，但政治宣传仍可能在网上迅速传播。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The researchers will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=publish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>publish</strong></a> a study of how groups disseminate propaganda through social media.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究者将发表一项关于团体如何通过社交媒体传播政治宣传的研究。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The researchers will publish a study of how groups <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disseminate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>disseminate</strong></a> propaganda through social media.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究者将发表一项关于团体如何通过社交媒体传播政治宣传的研究。</p></li>

### 词群 09.02｜人们怎样期待和享受娱乐 {#group-09-02}

**串联方式：** 由预期、等待进入休闲和消遣，再看节庆、节目、模仿与模拟；gossip 和 rumour 接到传播话题。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-019">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-019" class="vocabulary-word-title">foresee<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-019" aria-label="定位 foresee">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-019" data-mark-label="foresee" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="foresee：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="foresee：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=foresee&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 foresee" title="在有道查询 foresee">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-019</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 预见，预知</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We expect a large audience and anticipate delays, but we cannot <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=foresee&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>foresee</strong></a> every problem as we await approval.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们预计观众很多，也预料会有延误，但等待批准时无法预见所有问题。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Organisers await the forecast, expect rain and anticipate changes that few guests can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=foresee&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>foresee</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">组织者等待天气预报，预计会下雨，也预料会有许多宾客无法预见的调整。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As we await the ticket figures, we expect demand to rise and anticipate the crowding problems we can already <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=foresee&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>foresee</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">等待售票数据时，我们预计需求会上升，并提前准备应对已经能够预见的拥挤问题。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">foresee a problem</span> · 预见问题</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prediction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">prediction</a></span> · 预测</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=preparation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">preparation</a></span> · 准备</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-020">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-020" class="vocabulary-word-title">anticipate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-020" aria-label="定位 anticipate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-020" data-mark-label="anticipate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="anticipate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="anticipate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=anticipate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 anticipate" title="在有道查询 anticipate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-020</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 预期，预料</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We expect a large audience and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=anticipate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>anticipate</strong></a> delays, but we cannot foresee every problem as we await approval.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们预计观众很多，也预料会有延误，但等待批准时无法预见所有问题。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Organisers await the forecast, expect rain and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=anticipate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>anticipate</strong></a> changes that few guests can foresee.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">组织者等待天气预报，预计会下雨，也预料会有许多宾客无法预见的调整。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As we await the ticket figures, we expect demand to rise and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=anticipate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>anticipate</strong></a> the crowding problems we can already foresee.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">等待售票数据时，我们预计需求会上升，并提前准备应对已经能够预见的拥挤问题。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">anticipate demand</span> · 预估需求</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prediction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">prediction</a></span> · 预测</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=preparation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">preparation</a></span> · 准备</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-021">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-021" class="vocabulary-word-title">expect<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-021" aria-label="定位 expect">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-021" data-mark-label="expect" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="expect：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="expect：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=expect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 expect" title="在有道查询 expect">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-021</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 预料，预期</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=expect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>expect</strong></a> a large audience and anticipate delays, but we cannot foresee every problem as we await approval.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们预计观众很多，也预料会有延误，但等待批准时无法预见所有问题。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Organisers await the forecast, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=expect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>expect</strong></a> rain and anticipate changes that few guests can foresee.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">组织者等待天气预报，预计会下雨，也预料会有许多宾客无法预见的调整。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As we await the ticket figures, we <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=expect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>expect</strong></a> demand to rise and anticipate the crowding problems we can already foresee.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">等待售票数据时，我们预计需求会上升，并提前准备应对已经能够预见的拥挤问题。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">expect an increase</span> · 预期增长</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prediction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">prediction</a></span> · 预测</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=preparation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">preparation</a></span> · 准备</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-022">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-022" class="vocabulary-word-title">await<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-022" aria-label="定位 await">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-022" data-mark-label="await" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="await：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="await：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=await&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 await" title="在有道查询 await">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-022</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 等候，期待；将降临到……身上</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We expect a large audience and anticipate delays, but we cannot foresee every problem as we <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=await&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>await</strong></a> approval.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们预计观众很多，也预料会有延误，但等待批准时无法预见所有问题。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Organisers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=await&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>await</strong></a> the forecast, expect rain and anticipate changes that few guests can foresee.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">组织者等待天气预报，预计会下雨，也预料会有许多宾客无法预见的调整。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As we <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=await&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>await</strong></a> the ticket figures, we expect demand to rise and anticipate the crowding problems we can already foresee.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">等待售票数据时，我们预计需求会上升，并提前准备应对已经能够预见的拥挤问题。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">await a decision</span> · 等待决定</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prediction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">prediction</a></span> · 预测</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=preparation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">preparation</a></span> · 准备</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-023">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-023" class="vocabulary-word-title">pastime<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-023" aria-label="定位 pastime">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-023" data-mark-label="pastime" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="pastime：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="pastime：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pastime&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 pastime" title="在有道查询 pastime">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-023</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 消遣，娱乐</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Storytelling is a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pastime&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pastime</strong></a> that can entertain adults and amuse children while providing inexpensive recreation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">讲故事是一种能娱乐成人、逗乐儿童的消遣，也提供了廉价休闲。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For family recreation, choose a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pastime&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pastime</strong></a> that will amuse the children and entertain their grandparents.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">家庭休闲可以选择既逗孩子开心又让祖父母愉快的消遣。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A simple game can amuse visitors, entertain a crowd and turn a quiet <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pastime&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pastime</strong></a> into shared recreation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一个简单游戏可以逗乐访客、娱乐众人，把安静消遣变成共同休闲。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a favourite pastime</span> · 最喜欢的消遣</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=leisure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">leisure</a></span> · 闲暇</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hobby&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hobby</a></span> · 爱好</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-024">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.娱乐；招待；心存，怀有</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Storytelling is a pastime that can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=entertain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>entertain</strong></a> adults and amuse children while providing inexpensive recreation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">讲故事是一种能娱乐成人、逗乐儿童的消遣，也提供了廉价休闲。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For family recreation, choose a pastime that will amuse the children and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=entertain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>entertain</strong></a> their grandparents.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">家庭休闲可以选择既逗孩子开心又让祖父母愉快的消遣。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A simple game can amuse visitors, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=entertain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>entertain</strong></a> a crowd and turn a quiet pastime into shared recreation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一个简单游戏可以逗乐访客、娱乐众人，把安静消遣变成共同休闲。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">entertain guests</span> · 招待客人</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=leisure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">leisure</a></span> · 闲暇</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hobby&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hobby</a></span> · 爱好</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-025">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 休闲，娱乐</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Storytelling is a pastime that can entertain adults and amuse children while providing inexpensive <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recreation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>recreation</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">讲故事是一种能娱乐成人、逗乐儿童的消遣，也提供了廉价休闲。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For family <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recreation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>recreation</strong></a>, choose a pastime that will amuse the children and entertain their grandparents.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">家庭休闲可以选择既逗孩子开心又让祖父母愉快的消遣。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A simple game can amuse visitors, entertain a crowd and turn a quiet pastime into shared <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recreation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>recreation</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一个简单游戏可以逗乐访客、娱乐众人，把安静消遣变成共同休闲。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">outdoor recreation</span> · 户外休闲</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=leisure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">leisure</a></span> · 闲暇</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hobby&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hobby</a></span> · 爱好</li></ul>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Storytelling is a pastime that can entertain adults and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=amuse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>amuse</strong></a> children while providing inexpensive recreation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">讲故事是一种能娱乐成人、逗乐儿童的消遣，也提供了廉价休闲。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For family recreation, choose a pastime that will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=amuse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>amuse</strong></a> the children and entertain their grandparents.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">家庭休闲可以选择既逗孩子开心又让祖父母愉快的消遣。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A simple game can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=amuse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>amuse</strong></a> visitors, entertain a crowd and turn a quiet pastime into shared recreation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一个简单游戏可以逗乐访客、娱乐众人，把安静消遣变成共同休闲。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-027">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-027" class="vocabulary-word-title">gossip<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-027" aria-label="定位 gossip">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-027" data-mark-label="gossip" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="gossip：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="gossip：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gossip&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 gossip" title="在有道查询 gossip">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-027</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 闲聊；流言蜚语</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The group reached a consensus that the rumour was <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gossip&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gossip</strong></a> rather than reliable news.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">大家一致认为那个传闻只是闲言碎语，而非可靠消息。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gossip&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Gossip</strong></a> about the singer spread, but there was no consensus on whether the rumour was true.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">关于歌手的流言四起，但人们对传闻是否属实没有达成共识。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The editor sought a consensus before printing a rumour based only on backstage <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gossip&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gossip</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">编辑在刊登一条仅来自后台闲谈的传闻前，先征求了一致意见。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">spread gossip</span> · 传播闲言碎语</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hearsay&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hearsay</a></span> · 道听途说</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=verification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">verification</a></span> · 核实</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-028">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-028" class="vocabulary-word-title">rumour<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-028" aria-label="定位 rumour">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-028" data-mark-label="rumour" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="rumour：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="rumour：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rumour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 rumour" title="在有道查询 rumour">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-028</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">(= rumor)；n. 谣言，传闻</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The group reached a consensus that the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rumour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rumour</strong></a> was gossip rather than reliable news.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">大家一致认为那个传闻只是闲言碎语，而非可靠消息。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Gossip about the singer spread, but there was no consensus on whether the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rumour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rumour</strong></a> was true.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">关于歌手的流言四起，但人们对传闻是否属实没有达成共识。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The editor sought a consensus before printing a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rumour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rumour</strong></a> based only on backstage gossip.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">编辑在刊登一条仅来自后台闲谈的传闻前，先征求了一致意见。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an unfounded rumour</span> · 无根据的传闻</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hearsay&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hearsay</a></span> · 道听途说</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=verification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">verification</a></span> · 核实</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-029">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-029" class="vocabulary-word-title">consensus<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-029" aria-label="定位 consensus">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-029" data-mark-label="consensus" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="consensus：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="consensus：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consensus&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 consensus" title="在有道查询 consensus">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-029</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 共识，一致的意见</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The group reached a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consensus&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>consensus</strong></a> that the rumour was gossip rather than reliable news.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">大家一致认为那个传闻只是闲言碎语，而非可靠消息。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Gossip about the singer spread, but there was no <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consensus&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>consensus</strong></a> on whether the rumour was true.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">关于歌手的流言四起，但人们对传闻是否属实没有达成共识。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The editor sought a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consensus&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>consensus</strong></a> before printing a rumour based only on backstage gossip.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">编辑在刊登一条仅来自后台闲谈的传闻前，先征求了一致意见。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">reach a consensus</span> · 达成共识</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hearsay&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hearsay</a></span> · 道听途说</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=verification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">verification</a></span> · 核实</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-030">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-030" class="vocabulary-word-title">festival<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-030" aria-label="定位 festival">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-030" data-mark-label="festival" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="festival：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="festival：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=festival&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 festival" title="在有道查询 festival">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-030</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.节日；(音乐、电影等的)节</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=festival&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>festival</strong></a> programme ends with a feast in the village square.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">节日活动安排以村广场上的盛宴结束。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A community feast is the highlight of this year’s <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=festival&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>festival</strong></a> programme.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">社区盛宴是今年节日活动安排的亮点。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The programme lists the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=festival&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>festival</strong></a> concerts and gives the time of the evening feast.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">节目单列出了节日音乐会，并注明晚间盛宴的时间。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a music festival</span> · 音乐节</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=celebration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">celebration</a></span> · 庆典</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=schedule&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">schedule</a></span> · 日程</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-031">

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A community <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=feast&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>feast</strong></a> is the highlight of this year’s festival programme.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">社区盛宴是今年节日活动安排的亮点。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The programme lists the festival concerts and gives the time of the evening <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=feast&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>feast</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">节目单列出了节日音乐会，并注明晚间盛宴的时间。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The festival <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=programme&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>programme</strong></a> ends with a feast in the village square.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">节日活动安排以村广场上的盛宴结束。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A community feast is the highlight of this year’s festival <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=programme&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>programme</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">社区盛宴是今年节日活动安排的亮点。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=programme&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>programme</strong></a> lists the festival concerts and gives the time of the evening feast.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">节目单列出了节日音乐会，并注明晚间盛宴的时间。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-033">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-033" class="vocabulary-word-title">imitate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-033" aria-label="定位 imitate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-033" data-mark-label="imitate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="imitate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="imitate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=imitate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 imitate" title="在有道查询 imitate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-033</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 模仿，效仿</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Actors <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=imitate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>imitate</strong></a> everyday behaviour, mimic familiar voices and simulate emotions on stage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">演员在舞台上模仿日常行为、模仿熟悉的声音并模拟情感。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exercise asks students to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=imitate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>imitate</strong></a> a presenter, mimic the rhythm of speech and simulate a live interview.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">练习要求学生模仿主持人及其说话节奏，并模拟现场采访。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A robot can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=imitate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>imitate</strong></a> a gesture and mimic a voice to simulate a human conversation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">机器人可以模仿手势和声音，模拟人类对话。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">imitate a style</span> · 模仿风格</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=imitation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">imitation</a></span> · 模仿</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=simulation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">simulation</a></span> · 模拟</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-034">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-034" class="vocabulary-word-title">mimic<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-034" aria-label="定位 mimic">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-034" data-mark-label="mimic" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="mimic：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="mimic：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mimic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 mimic" title="在有道查询 mimic">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-034</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.模仿，效仿</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Actors imitate everyday behaviour, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mimic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mimic</strong></a> familiar voices and simulate emotions on stage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">演员在舞台上模仿日常行为、模仿熟悉的声音并模拟情感。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exercise asks students to imitate a presenter, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mimic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mimic</strong></a> the rhythm of speech and simulate a live interview.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">练习要求学生模仿主持人及其说话节奏，并模拟现场采访。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A robot can imitate a gesture and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mimic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mimic</strong></a> a voice to simulate a human conversation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">机器人可以模仿手势和声音，模拟人类对话。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">mimic a voice</span> · 模仿声音</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=imitation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">imitation</a></span> · 模仿</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=simulation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">simulation</a></span> · 模拟</li></ul>
</div>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-035">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-035" class="vocabulary-word-title">simulate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-035" aria-label="定位 simulate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-035" data-mark-label="simulate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="simulate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="simulate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=simulate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 simulate" title="在有道查询 simulate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-035</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.模拟；假装，冒充</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Actors imitate everyday behaviour, mimic familiar voices and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=simulate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>simulate</strong></a> emotions on stage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">演员在舞台上模仿日常行为、模仿熟悉的声音并模拟情感。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exercise asks students to imitate a presenter, mimic the rhythm of speech and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=simulate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>simulate</strong></a> a live interview.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">练习要求学生模仿主持人及其说话节奏，并模拟现场采访。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A robot can imitate a gesture and mimic a voice to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=simulate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>simulate</strong></a> a human conversation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">机器人可以模仿手势和声音，模拟人类对话。</p></li>
</ol>

</div>
</details>

### 词群 09.03｜选择一种表演或影像形式 {#group-09-03}
