<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-039">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-039" class="vocabulary-word-title">concert<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-039" aria-label="定位 concert">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-039" data-mark-label="concert" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="concert：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="concert：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=concert&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 concert" title="在有道查询 concert">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-039</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 音乐会</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The orchestra’s <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=concert&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>concert</strong></a> features a symphony and music from ballet and opera.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">管弦乐队的音乐会包含一部交响曲，以及芭蕾舞剧和歌剧选段。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The opera house hosted a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=concert&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>concert</strong></a> in which the orchestra played a symphony before accompanying the ballet dancers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">歌剧院举办了一场音乐会，管弦乐队先演奏一部交响曲，再为芭蕾舞者伴奏。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At the outdoor <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=concert&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>concert</strong></a>, the orchestra moved from a symphony to familiar ballet and opera melodies.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">户外音乐会上，管弦乐队从交响曲转而演奏熟悉的芭蕾和歌剧旋律。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a live concert</span> · 现场音乐会</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conductor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">conductor</a></span> · 指挥</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=score&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">score</a></span> · 乐谱</li></ul>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students can adapt a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=comedy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>comedy</strong></a> or a tragedy using stop-motion animation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生可以用定格动画改编喜剧或悲剧。</p></li>

</ol>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The cinema will not admit children to an X-rated movie, even during its <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=film&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>film</strong></a> festival.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">即使在电影节期间，这家电影院也不允许儿童观看成人级电影。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=film&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>film</strong></a> guide clearly marks the movie as X-rated.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">电影指南明确将这部电影标为成人级。</p></li>

</ol>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibition brought together an experimental <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=artist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>artist</strong></a>, a furniture craftsman and a portrait painter.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览汇集了一位实验艺术家、一位家具工匠和一位肖像画家。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=role&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>role</strong></a> required her to remain on stage throughout the opening scene.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她扮演的角色要求她在整个开场中留在舞台上。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The actor stepped onto the stage to rehearse a difficult scene in his new <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=role&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>role</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">演员走上舞台，排练新角色的一场难戏。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Changing the stage lighting gave the final scene a more dramatic <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=role&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>role</strong></a> in the production.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">改变舞台灯光，使最后一场在整部作品中起到了更强的戏剧作用。</p></li>
</ol>

<summary><h4 id="word-09-057" class="vocabulary-word-title">exhibition<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-057" aria-label="定位 exhibition">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-057" data-mark-label="exhibition" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="exhibition：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="exhibition：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exhibition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 exhibition" title="在有道查询 exhibition">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-057</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.展览</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The gallery arranged the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exhibition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>exhibition</strong></a> to highlight the aesthetic appeal of everyday objects.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">美术馆布置展览，突出日常物品的审美吸引力。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exhibition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>exhibition</strong></a> challenged the gallery’s usual aesthetic standards.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这次展览挑战了美术馆惯常的审美标准。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Visitors praised the aesthetic unity of the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exhibition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>exhibition</strong></a> in the new gallery.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">观众称赞了新美术馆展览的审美统一性。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a temporary exhibition</span> · 临时展览</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=curator&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">curator</a></span> · 策展人</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=artwork&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">artwork</a></span> · 艺术作品</li></ul>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The gallery arranged the exhibition to highlight the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aesthetic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>aesthetic</strong></a> appeal of everyday objects.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">美术馆布置展览，突出日常物品的审美吸引力。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibition challenged the gallery’s usual <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aesthetic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>aesthetic</strong></a> standards.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这次展览挑战了美术馆惯常的审美标准。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Visitors praised the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aesthetic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>aesthetic</strong></a> unity of the exhibition in the new gallery.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">观众称赞了新美术馆展览的审美统一性。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-059">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-059" class="vocabulary-word-title">collect<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-059" aria-label="定位 collect">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-059" data-mark-label="collect" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="collect：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="collect：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=collect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 collect" title="在有道查询 collect">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-059</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.收集；收藏</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Collectors who <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=collect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>collect</strong></a> local art may select a small painting or opt for a sculpture.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">收藏本地艺术品的藏家可以挑一幅小画，或选择一件雕塑。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=collect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>collect</strong></a> photographs, select a theme and opt for prints you can store safely.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">收藏照片前，先选定主题，再选择能安全存放的照片。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Museums <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=collect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>collect</strong></a> many objects but select only a few for display and often opt for a rotating exhibition.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆收藏许多物品，却只选少量展出，且常选择轮换展览。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">collect art</span> · 收藏艺术品</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=collection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">collection</a></span> · 藏品</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=acquisition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">acquisition</a></span> · 购藏</li></ul>
</div>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-060">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-060" class="vocabulary-word-title">select<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-060" aria-label="定位 select">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-060" data-mark-label="select" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="select：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="select：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=select&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 select" title="在有道查询 select">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-060</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.选择，挑选</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Collectors who collect local art may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=select&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>select</strong></a> a small painting or opt for a sculpture.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">收藏本地艺术品的藏家可以挑一幅小画，或选择一件雕塑。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you collect photographs, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=select&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>select</strong></a> a theme and opt for prints you can store safely.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">收藏照片前，先选定主题，再选择能安全存放的照片。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Museums collect many objects but <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=select&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>select</strong></a> only a few for display and often opt for a rotating exhibition.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆收藏许多物品，却只选少量展出，且常选择轮换展览。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=collection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">collection</a></span> · 藏品</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=acquisition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">acquisition</a></span> · 购藏</li></ul>

### 词群 09.05｜视觉艺术怎样创作 {#group-09-05}

**串联方式：** 从照片、肖像、绘画和雕塑进入描绘、描述、雕刻与即兴创作。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-062">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-062" class="vocabulary-word-title">photograph<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-062" aria-label="定位 photograph">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-062" data-mark-label="photograph" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="photograph：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="photograph：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=photograph&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 photograph" title="在有道查询 photograph">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-062</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.照片，相片</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=photograph&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>photograph</strong></a> shows a portrait painting beside a bronze sculpture.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这张照片展示了一幅肖像油画和旁边的一件青铜雕塑。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The gallery acquired a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=photograph&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>photograph</strong></a>, a portrait painting and a small sculpture from the same artist.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">美术馆购藏了同一艺术家的一张照片、一幅肖像画和一件小雕塑。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For her portrait project, she compared a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=photograph&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>photograph</strong></a> with a painting and a sculpture of the same person.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为完成肖像项目，她比较了同一个人的照片、绘画和雕塑。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">take a photograph</span> · 拍照片</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=likeness&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">likeness</a></span> · 相貌的再现</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=composition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">composition</a></span> · 构图</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-063">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-063" class="vocabulary-word-title">portrait<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-063" aria-label="定位 portrait">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-063" data-mark-label="portrait" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="portrait：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="portrait：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=portrait&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 portrait" title="在有道查询 portrait">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-063</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.肖像，半身画像</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The photograph shows a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=portrait&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>portrait</strong></a> painting beside a bronze sculpture.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这张照片展示了一幅肖像油画和旁边的一件青铜雕塑。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The gallery acquired a photograph, a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=portrait&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>portrait</strong></a> painting and a small sculpture from the same artist.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">美术馆购藏了同一艺术家的一张照片、一幅肖像画和一件小雕塑。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For her <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=portrait&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>portrait</strong></a> project, she compared a photograph with a painting and a sculpture of the same person.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为完成肖像项目，她比较了同一个人的照片、绘画和雕塑。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-064">

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.油画，绘画；</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The photograph shows a portrait <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=painting&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>painting</strong></a> beside a bronze sculpture.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这张照片展示了一幅肖像油画和旁边的一件青铜雕塑。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The gallery acquired a photograph, a portrait <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=painting&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>painting</strong></a> and a small sculpture from the same artist.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">美术馆购藏了同一艺术家的一张照片、一幅肖像画和一件小雕塑。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For her portrait project, she compared a photograph with a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=painting&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>painting</strong></a> and a sculpture of the same person.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为完成肖像项目，她比较了同一个人的照片、绘画和雕塑。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">With limited tools, the artist had to improvise a way to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=carve&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>carve</strong></a> the wood.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">由于工具有限，艺术家只好临时想办法雕刻木头。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">You can improvise a simple design before you <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=carve&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>carve</strong></a> the final pattern.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">雕刻最终图案之前，你可以先即兴设计一个简单样式。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The workshop teaches visitors to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=carve&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>carve</strong></a> soft stone and improvise when the material cracks.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工作坊教访客雕刻软石，以及材料裂开时如何临机应变。</p></li>
</ol>

</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-070">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.临时做；即兴创作</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">With limited tools, the artist had to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=improvise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>improvise</strong></a> a way to carve the wood.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">由于工具有限，艺术家只好临时想办法雕刻木头。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">You can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=improvise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>improvise</strong></a> a simple design before you carve the final pattern.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">雕刻最终图案之前，你可以先即兴设计一个简单样式。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The workshop teaches visitors to carve soft stone and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=improvise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>improvise</strong></a> when the material cracks.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工作坊教访客雕刻软石，以及材料裂开时如何临机应变。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">improvise a scene</span> · 即兴演一场戏</p>

### 词群 09.06｜一首音乐由什么构成 {#group-09-06}

**串联方式：** 先辨音乐风格，再听歌词、乐队与独奏，最后关注旋律、节奏和音调。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-071">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-071" class="vocabulary-word-title">musical<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-071" aria-label="定位 musical">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-071" data-mark-label="musical" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="musical：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="musical：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=musical&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 musical" title="在有道查询 musical">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-071</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.音乐的；悦耳的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=musical&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>musical</strong></a> taste ranges from classical music to jazz and rock.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她的音乐品味涵盖古典音乐、爵士乐和摇滚乐。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=musical&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>musical</strong></a> festival pairs a classical ensemble with jazz players and a rock band.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这个音乐节安排古典乐团与爵士乐手、摇滚乐队同台。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">His <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=musical&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>musical</strong></a> training in classical piano influenced both his jazz and rock recordings.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他的古典钢琴训练影响了他录制的爵士乐和摇滚乐。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-075">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.嘻哈文化</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The band mixed <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hip-hop&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hip-hop</strong></a> and pop, with each lyric clearly printed in the programme.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">乐队融合嘻哈与流行音乐，每句歌词都清楚印在节目单上。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A memorable lyric helped the band bring <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hip-hop&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hip-hop</strong></a> influences into its pop songs.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一句难忘的歌词帮助乐队把嘻哈元素带进流行歌曲。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The band invited a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hip-hop&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hip-hop</strong></a> artist to rewrite a lyric for its new pop single.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">乐队邀请一位嘻哈艺人为新的流行单曲改写一句歌词。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">hip-hop culture</span> · 嘻哈文化</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rap</a></span> · 说唱</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=single&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">single</a></span> · 单曲</li></ul>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=band&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>band</strong></a> mixed hip-hop and pop, with each lyric clearly printed in the programme.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">乐队融合嘻哈与流行音乐，每句歌词都清楚印在节目单上。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A memorable lyric helped the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=band&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>band</strong></a> bring hip-hop influences into its pop songs.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一句难忘的歌词帮助乐队把嘻哈元素带进流行歌曲。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=band&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>band</strong></a> invited a hip-hop artist to rewrite a lyric for its new pop single.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">乐队邀请一位嘻哈艺人为新的流行单曲改写一句歌词。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a live band</span> · 现场乐队</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">rap</a></span> · 说唱</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=single&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">single</a></span> · 单曲</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-079">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-079" class="vocabulary-word-title">solo<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-079" aria-label="定位 solo">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-079" data-mark-label="solo" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="solo：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="solo：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=solo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 solo" title="在有道查询 solo">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-079</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n独奏，独唱</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The piano <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=solo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>solo</strong></a> introduces the main melody of the tune.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">钢琴独奏引出了这首曲调的主旋律。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She played a familiar tune as a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=solo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>solo</strong></a>, adding ornaments to the melody.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她独奏了一首熟悉的曲子，在旋律中加了装饰音。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=solo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>solo</strong></a> changes the melody slightly while keeping the tune recognisable.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">独奏稍稍改变了旋律，但仍能辨认出原曲。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a piano solo</span> · 钢琴独奏</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=theme&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">theme</a></span> · 主题旋律</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=improvisation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">improvisation</a></span> · 即兴创作</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-080">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-080" class="vocabulary-word-title">melody<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-080" aria-label="定位 melody">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-080" data-mark-label="melody" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="melody：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="melody：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=melody&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 melody" title="在有道查询 melody">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-080</span></span></summary>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The piano solo introduces the main <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=melody&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>melody</strong></a> of the tune.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">钢琴独奏引出了这首曲调的主旋律。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She played a familiar tune as a solo, adding ornaments to the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=melody&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>melody</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她独奏了一首熟悉的曲子，在旋律中加了装饰音。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The solo changes the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=melody&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>melody</strong></a> slightly while keeping the tune recognisable.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">独奏稍稍改变了旋律，但仍能辨认出原曲。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-081">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-081" class="vocabulary-word-title">rhythm<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-081" aria-label="定位 rhythm">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-081" data-mark-label="rhythm" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="rhythm：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="rhythm：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rhythm&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 rhythm" title="在有道查询 rhythm">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-081</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n节奏，韵律</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The disc captures the drum’s steady <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rhythm&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rhythm</strong></a> and the trumpet’s warm tone.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这张光盘记录了鼓的稳定节奏和小号的温暖音色。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Listen to the disc and compare the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rhythm&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rhythm</strong></a> and tone of the two recordings.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">听这张光盘，比较两段录音的节奏与音色。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The remastered disc preserves the original <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rhythm&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rhythm</strong></a> while improving the clarity of every tone.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">重新制作的光盘保留了原有节奏，并让每个音色更清晰。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a steady rhythm</span> · 稳定节奏</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recording&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">recording</a></span> · 录音</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sound%20quality&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sound quality</a></span> · 音质</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-082">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-082" class="vocabulary-word-title">tone<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-082" aria-label="定位 tone">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-082" data-mark-label="tone" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="tone：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="tone：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tone&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 tone" title="在有道查询 tone">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-082</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.音色；声调；腔调</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The disc captures the drum’s steady rhythm and the trumpet’s warm <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tone&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tone</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这张光盘记录了鼓的稳定节奏和小号的温暖音色。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Listen to the disc and compare the rhythm and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tone&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tone</strong></a> of the two recordings.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">听这张光盘，比较两段录音的节奏与音色。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The remastered disc preserves the original rhythm while improving the clarity of every <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tone&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tone</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">重新制作的光盘保留了原有节奏，并让每个音色更清晰。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a warm tone</span> · 温暖音色</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recording&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">recording</a></span> · 录音</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sound%20quality&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sound quality</a></span> · 音质</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-083">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The piano solo introduces the main melody of the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tune&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tune</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">钢琴独奏引出了这首曲调的主旋律。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She played a familiar <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tune&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tune</strong></a> as a solo, adding ornaments to the melody.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她独奏了一首熟悉的曲子，在旋律中加了装饰音。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The solo changes the melody slightly while keeping the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tune&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tune</strong></a> recognisable.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">独奏稍稍改变了旋律，但仍能辨认出原曲。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a catchy tune</span> · 朗朗上口的曲调</p>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disc&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>disc</strong></a> captures the drum’s steady rhythm and the trumpet’s warm tone.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这张光盘记录了鼓的稳定节奏和小号的温暖音色。</p></li>

### 词群 09.07｜辨认乐器家族 {#group-09-07}

**串联方式：** 把键盘、弦乐、管乐与打击乐分组，想象一支乐队如何配置。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-085">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-085" class="vocabulary-word-title">piano<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-085" aria-label="定位 piano">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-085" data-mark-label="piano" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="piano：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="piano：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=piano&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 piano" title="在有道查询 piano">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-085</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.钢琴</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The arrangement combines <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=piano&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>piano</strong></a>, violin, cello and guitar.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这份编曲融合了钢琴、小提琴、大提琴和吉他。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She plays <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=piano&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>piano</strong></a> and guitar, while her brothers study violin and cello.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她弹钢琴和吉他，而哥哥们学小提琴和大提琴。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.口琴</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The street band features <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=harmonica&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>harmonica</strong></a>, trumpet, drum and flute.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">街头乐队使用口琴、小号、鼓和长笛。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A drum beat supported the trumpet solo while the flute and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=harmonica&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>harmonica</strong></a> added soft harmonies.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">鼓点托着小号独奏，长笛和口琴则加入柔和的和声。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Children could try a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=harmonica&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>harmonica</strong></a> or flute before watching the trumpet player and drum instructor perform.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">孩子们可以先试口琴或长笛，再观看小号乐手和鼓乐老师表演。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Children could try a harmonica or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flute</strong></a> before watching the trumpet player and drum instructor perform.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">孩子们可以先试口琴或长笛，再观看小号乐手和鼓乐老师表演。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a bamboo flute</span> · 竹笛</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wind%20instrument&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">wind instrument</a></span> · 管乐器</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=percussion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">percussion</a></span> · 打击乐器</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 09.08｜比赛由谁组织和参与 {#group-09-08}

**串联方式：** 从赛事、赞助与支持者进入运动员、冠军和观众，再描述动作和知名度；pitch 在运动中可指场地或投球。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-093">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-093" class="vocabulary-word-title">competition<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-093" aria-label="定位 competition">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-093" data-mark-label="competition" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="competition：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="competition：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=competition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 competition" title="在有道查询 competition">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-093</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.竞争；比赛</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An Olympic champion became patron of the tournament, while a local company agreed to sponsor the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=competition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>competition</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位奥运冠军担任锦标赛的赞助支持者，一家本地公司同意赞助赛事。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The sponsor invited the Olympic team to meet the tournament patron before the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=competition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>competition</strong></a> began.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">比赛开始前，赞助商邀请奥运代表队见锦标赛的赞助支持者。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Without a sponsor or patron, the tournament could not offer the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=competition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>competition</strong></a> facilities expected by Olympic athletes.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果没有赞助商或赞助人，锦标赛就无法提供奥运运动员期望的比赛设施。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">enter a competition</span> · 参加比赛</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=funding&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">funding</a></span> · 资金支持</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sporting%20event&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sporting event</a></span> · 体育赛事</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An Olympic champion became patron of the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tournament&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tournament</strong></a>, while a local company agreed to sponsor the competition.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位奥运冠军担任锦标赛的赞助支持者，一家本地公司同意赞助赛事。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The sponsor invited the Olympic team to meet the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tournament&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tournament</strong></a> patron before the competition began.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">比赛开始前，赞助商邀请奥运代表队见锦标赛的赞助支持者。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Without a sponsor or patron, the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tournament&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tournament</strong></a> could not offer the competition facilities expected by Olympic athletes.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果没有赞助商或赞助人，锦标赛就无法提供奥运运动员期望的比赛设施。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a tennis tournament</span> · 网球锦标赛</p>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Olympic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Olympic</strong></a> champion became patron of the tournament, while a local company agreed to sponsor the competition.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位奥运冠军担任锦标赛的赞助支持者，一家本地公司同意赞助赛事。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The sponsor invited the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Olympic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Olympic</strong></a> team to meet the tournament patron before the competition began.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">比赛开始前，赞助商邀请奥运代表队见锦标赛的赞助支持者。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Without a sponsor or patron, the tournament could not offer the competition facilities expected by <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Olympic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Olympic</strong></a> athletes.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果没有赞助商或赞助人，锦标赛就无法提供奥运运动员期望的比赛设施。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sponsor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sponsor</strong></a> invited the Olympic team to meet the tournament patron before the competition began.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">比赛开始前，赞助商邀请奥运代表队见锦标赛的赞助支持者。</p></li>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An Olympic champion became <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=patron&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>patron</strong></a> of the tournament, while a local company agreed to sponsor the competition.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位奥运冠军担任锦标赛的赞助支持者，一家本地公司同意赞助赛事。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The sponsor invited the Olympic team to meet the tournament <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=patron&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>patron</strong></a> before the competition began.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">比赛开始前，赞助商邀请奥运代表队见锦标赛的赞助支持者。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Without a sponsor or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=patron&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>patron</strong></a>, the tournament could not offer the competition facilities expected by Olympic athletes.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果没有赞助商或赞助人，锦标赛就无法提供奥运运动员期望的比赛设施。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-098">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-098" class="vocabulary-word-title">athlete<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-098" aria-label="定位 athlete">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-098" data-mark-label="athlete" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="athlete：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="athlete：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=athlete&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 athlete" title="在有道查询 athlete">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-098</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.运动员:［英］田径运动员</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A volunteer helped a young spectator meet the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=athlete&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>athlete</strong></a> who had become world champion.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">志愿者帮助一位年轻观众见到了那位成为世界冠军的运动员。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The champion thanked every volunteer and spectator who had supported her as a young <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=athlete&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>athlete</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">冠军感谢了她年轻时作为运动员受到的每位志愿者和观众的支持。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=athlete&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>athlete</strong></a> passed the spectator stand, where a volunteer held a banner for the defending champion.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">每位运动员经过观众看台时，一位志愿者正在那里举着支持卫冕冠军的横幅。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an elite athlete</span> · 精英运动员</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=supporter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">supporter</a></span> · 支持者</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=medallist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">medallist</a></span> · 奖牌获得者</li></ul>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.冠军，第一名</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A volunteer helped a young spectator meet the athlete who had become world <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=champion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>champion</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">志愿者帮助一位年轻观众见到了那位成为世界冠军的运动员。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=champion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>champion</strong></a> thanked every volunteer and spectator who had supported her as a young athlete.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">冠军感谢了她年轻时作为运动员受到的每位志愿者和观众的支持。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Each athlete passed the spectator stand, where a volunteer held a banner for the defending <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=champion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>champion</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">每位运动员经过观众看台时，一位志愿者正在那里举着支持卫冕冠军的横幅。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a world champion</span> · 世界冠军</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=supporter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">supporter</a></span> · 支持者</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=medallist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">medallist</a></span> · 奖牌获得者</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A volunteer helped a young <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spectator&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spectator</strong></a> meet the athlete who had become world champion.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">志愿者帮助一位年轻观众见到了那位成为世界冠军的运动员。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The champion thanked every volunteer and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spectator&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spectator</strong></a> who had supported her as a young athlete.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">冠军感谢了她年轻时作为运动员受到的每位志愿者和观众的支持。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Each athlete passed the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spectator&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spectator</strong></a> stand, where a volunteer held a banner for the defending champion.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">每位运动员经过观众看台时，一位志愿者正在那里举着支持卫冕冠军的横幅。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-101">

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=volunteer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>volunteer</strong></a> helped a young spectator meet the athlete who had become world champion.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">志愿者帮助一位年轻观众见到了那位成为世界冠军的运动员。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The champion thanked every <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=volunteer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>volunteer</strong></a> and spectator who had supported her as a young athlete.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">冠军感谢了她年轻时作为运动员受到的每位志愿者和观众的支持。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Each athlete passed the spectator stand, where a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=volunteer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>volunteer</strong></a> held a banner for the defending champion.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">每位运动员经过观众看台时，一位志愿者正在那里举着支持卫冕冠军的横幅。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-102">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-102" class="vocabulary-word-title">motion<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-102" aria-label="定位 motion">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-102" data-mark-label="motion" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="motion：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="motion：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=motion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 motion" title="在有道查询 motion">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-102</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 运动；动作；提议（其他语境）</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The replay showed in slow <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=motion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>motion</strong></a> how the players left the pitch and took a bow.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">回放以慢动作展示了球员如何离开球场并鞠躬致谢。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After demonstrating the kicking <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=motion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>motion</strong></a> on the pitch, the coach gave a playful bow.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练在球场演示踢球动作后，开玩笑地鞠了一躬。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The camera captured the athlete’s bow at the edge of the pitch in slow <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=motion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>motion</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">摄像机以慢动作记录了运动员在球场边的鞠躬。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">slow motion</span> · 慢动作</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=replay&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">replay</a></span> · 回放</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gesture&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">gesture</a></span> · 动作手势</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-103">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-103" class="vocabulary-word-title">pitch<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-103" aria-label="定位 pitch">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-103" data-mark-label="pitch" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="pitch：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="pitch：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pitch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 pitch" title="在有道查询 pitch">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-103</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 运动场地；投球；音高；沥青（材料语境）</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The replay showed in slow motion how the players left the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pitch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pitch</strong></a> and took a bow.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">回放以慢动作展示了球员如何离开球场并鞠躬致谢。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After demonstrating the kicking motion on the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pitch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pitch</strong></a>, the coach gave a playful bow.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练在球场演示踢球动作后，开玩笑地鞠了一躬。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The camera captured the athlete’s bow at the edge of the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pitch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pitch</strong></a> in slow motion.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">摄像机以慢动作记录了运动员在球场边的鞠躬。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a football pitch</span> · 足球场</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=replay&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">replay</a></span> · 回放</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gesture&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">gesture</a></span> · 动作手势</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-104">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-104" class="vocabulary-word-title">bow<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-104" aria-label="定位 bow">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-104" data-mark-label="bow" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="bow：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="bow：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 bow" title="在有道查询 bow">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-104</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 鞠躬；n. 琴弓；弓；蝴蝶结（依语境）</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The replay showed in slow motion how the players left the pitch and took a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bow</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">回放以慢动作展示了球员如何离开球场并鞠躬致谢。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After demonstrating the kicking motion on the pitch, the coach gave a playful <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bow</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练在球场演示踢球动作后，开玩笑地鞠了一躬。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The camera captured the athlete’s <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bow</strong></a> at the edge of the pitch in slow motion.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">摄像机以慢动作记录了运动员在球场边的鞠躬。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">take a bow</span> · 鞠躬致谢</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=replay&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">replay</a></span> · 回放</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gesture&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">gesture</a></span> · 动作手势</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-105">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-105" class="vocabulary-word-title">famous<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-105" aria-label="定位 famous">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-105" data-mark-label="famous" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="famous：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="famous：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=famous&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 famous" title="在有道查询 famous">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-105</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.著名的，出名的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=famous&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>famous</strong></a> athlete trained with a well-known local coach.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">著名运动员曾跟随一位本地知名教练训练。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A well-known tournament can make a previously unknown player <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=famous&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>famous</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">知名赛事能让原本默默无闻的选手成名。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=famous&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>famous</strong></a> stadium is well-known for its enthusiastic crowds.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这座著名体育场以热情观众闻名。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a famous athlete</span> · 著名运动员</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reputation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reputation</a></span> · 声誉</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=celebrity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">celebrity</a></span> · 名人</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-106">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-106" class="vocabulary-word-title">well-known<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-106" aria-label="定位 well-known">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-106" data-mark-label="well-known" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="well-known：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="well-known：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=well-known&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 well-known" title="在有道查询 well-known">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-106</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.众所周知的，出名的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The famous athlete trained with a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=well-known&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>well-known</strong></a> local coach.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">著名运动员曾跟随一位本地知名教练训练。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=well-known&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>well-known</strong></a> tournament can make a previously unknown player famous.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">知名赛事能让原本默默无闻的选手成名。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Both <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=indoor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>indoor</strong></a> and outdoor exercise can form part of a balanced training routine.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">室内和户外运动都可以成为均衡训练的一部分。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">indoor facilities</span> · 室内设施</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=routine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">routine</a></span> · 常规安排</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conditioning&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">conditioning</a></span> · 体能训练</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The training plan combines indoor exercise with <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=outdoor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>outdoor</strong></a> running.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">训练计划把室内锻炼与户外跑步相结合。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Bad weather moved the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=outdoor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>outdoor</strong></a> training session into an indoor exercise area.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">恶劣天气使户外训练转移到了室内锻炼区。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Both indoor and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=outdoor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>outdoor</strong></a> exercise can form part of a balanced training routine.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">室内和户外运动都可以成为均衡训练的一部分。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">outdoor activities</span> · 户外活动</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=routine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">routine</a></span> · 常规安排</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conditioning&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">conditioning</a></span> · 体能训练</li></ul>
