<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-115">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-115" class="vocabulary-word-title">mobile<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-115" aria-label="定位 mobile">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-115" data-mark-label="mobile" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="mobile：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="mobile：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mobile&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 mobile" title="在有道查询 mobile">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-115</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 可移动的；行动方便的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Yoga encourages gentle movement that can help older people stay <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mobile&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mobile</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">瑜伽鼓励轻柔活动，有助于老年人保持行动灵活。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The instructor adapted each yoga movement for participants who were less <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mobile&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mobile</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练为行动不太方便的参与者调整了每一个瑜伽动作。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mobile&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mobile</strong></a> person may still need to practise slow movement when learning yoga.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">行动灵活的人学瑜伽时，也可能需要练习缓慢动作。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">stay mobile</span> · 保持行动灵活</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flexibility&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">flexibility</a></span> · 柔韧性</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=balance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">balance</a></span> · 平衡</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-116">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-116" class="vocabulary-word-title">movement<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-116" aria-label="定位 movement">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-116" data-mark-label="movement" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="movement：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="movement：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=movement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 movement" title="在有道查询 movement">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-116</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. (身体部位的)活动；运动；移动</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Yoga encourages gentle <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=movement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>movement</strong></a> that can help older people stay mobile.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">瑜伽鼓励轻柔活动，有助于老年人保持行动灵活。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The instructor adapted each yoga <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=movement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>movement</strong></a> for participants who were less mobile.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练为行动不太方便的参与者调整了每一个瑜伽动作。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A mobile person may still need to practise slow <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=movement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>movement</strong></a> when learning yoga.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">行动灵活的人学瑜伽时，也可能需要练习缓慢动作。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">freedom of movement</span> · 活动自由度</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flexibility&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">flexibility</a></span> · 柔韧性</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=balance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">balance</a></span> · 平衡</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-117">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-117" class="vocabulary-word-title">yoga<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-117" aria-label="定位 yoga">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-117" data-mark-label="yoga" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="yoga：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="yoga：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=yoga&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 yoga" title="在有道查询 yoga">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-117</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.瑜伽</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=yoga&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Yoga</strong></a> encourages gentle movement that can help older people stay mobile.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">瑜伽鼓励轻柔活动，有助于老年人保持行动灵活。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The instructor adapted each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=yoga&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>yoga</strong></a> movement for participants who were less mobile.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练为行动不太方便的参与者调整了每一个瑜伽动作。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A mobile person may still need to practise slow movement when learning <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=yoga&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>yoga</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">行动灵活的人学瑜伽时，也可能需要练习缓慢动作。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">practise yoga</span> · 练瑜伽</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flexibility&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">flexibility</a></span> · 柔韧性</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=balance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">balance</a></span> · 平衡</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-118">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-118" class="vocabulary-word-title">sprawl<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-118" aria-label="定位 sprawl">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-118" data-mark-label="sprawl" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="sprawl：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="sprawl：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sprawl&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 sprawl" title="在有道查询 sprawl">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-118</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.仰开四肢坐着（或躺着)；延伸，蔓延</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After the run, stretch gently before you <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sprawl&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sprawl</strong></a> on the grass, and stop if you feel a strain.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">跑步后先轻柔拉伸，再四肢摊开躺在草地上；感到拉伤时要停下来。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">You may strain your back if you <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sprawl&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sprawl</strong></a> awkwardly and then stretch too far.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果姿势别扭地摊躺着，又过度伸展，可能拉伤背部。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The coach told us to stretch slowly, avoid muscle strain and resist the urge to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sprawl&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sprawl</strong></a> on the cold floor.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练让我们缓慢拉伸、避免肌肉拉伤，并忍住躺倒在冷地板上的冲动。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">sprawl on the grass</span> · 四肢摊开躺在草地上</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=warm-up&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">warm-up</a></span> · 热身</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=posture&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">posture</a></span> · 姿势</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-119">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-119" class="vocabulary-word-title">stretch<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-119" aria-label="定位 stretch">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-119" data-mark-label="stretch" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="stretch：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="stretch：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stretch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 stretch" title="在有道查询 stretch">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-119</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.伸展；伸长（胳膊、腿)</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After the run, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stretch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stretch</strong></a> gently before you sprawl on the grass, and stop if you feel a strain.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">跑步后先轻柔拉伸，再四肢摊开躺在草地上；感到拉伤时要停下来。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">You may strain your back if you sprawl awkwardly and then <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stretch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stretch</strong></a> too far.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果姿势别扭地摊躺着，又过度伸展，可能拉伤背部。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The coach told us to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stretch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stretch</strong></a> slowly, avoid muscle strain and resist the urge to sprawl on the cold floor.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练让我们缓慢拉伸、避免肌肉拉伤，并忍住躺倒在冷地板上的冲动。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">stretch a muscle</span> · 拉伸肌肉</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=warm-up&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">warm-up</a></span> · 热身</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=posture&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">posture</a></span> · 姿势</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-120">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-120" class="vocabulary-word-title">strain<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-120" aria-label="定位 strain">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-120" data-mark-label="strain" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="strain：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="strain：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=strain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 strain" title="在有道查询 strain">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-120</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.拉紧：拉伤，扭伤；n.张力，拉力；(精神上的)紧张</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After the run, stretch gently before you sprawl on the grass, and stop if you feel a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=strain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>strain</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">跑步后先轻柔拉伸，再四肢摊开躺在草地上；感到拉伤时要停下来。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">You may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=strain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>strain</strong></a> your back if you sprawl awkwardly and then stretch too far.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果姿势别扭地摊躺着，又过度伸展，可能拉伤背部。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The coach told us to stretch slowly, avoid muscle <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=strain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>strain</strong></a> and resist the urge to sprawl on the cold floor.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练让我们缓慢拉伸、避免肌肉拉伤，并忍住躺倒在冷地板上的冲动。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">muscle strain</span> · 肌肉拉伤</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=warm-up&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">warm-up</a></span> · 热身</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=posture&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">posture</a></span> · 姿势</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She prefers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chess&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>chess</strong></a> or billiards, while her brother enjoys badminton and golf.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她更喜欢国际象棋或台球，而哥哥喜欢羽毛球和高尔夫。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The school offers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=soccer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>soccer</strong></a>, tennis and volleyball in summer, then hockey and indoor cricket in winter.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学校夏季开设足球、网球和排球，冬季则开设曲棍球和室内板球。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">He coaches <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=soccer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>soccer</strong></a> and hockey, while his colleagues teach tennis, volleyball and cricket.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他教足球和曲棍球，同事教网球、排球和板球。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students voted for <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=soccer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>soccer</strong></a> and volleyball, but the club also kept tennis, hockey and cricket.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生投票选择了足球和排球，但俱乐部也保留了网球、曲棍球和板球。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.敲，击；撞</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Flip a coin to choose who will throw first, then toss the ball gently so your partner can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=knock&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>knock</strong></a> it back.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">掷硬币决定谁先投球，然后轻轻抛球，让同伴把球击回来。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The drill asks you to flip the paddle, toss the ball, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=knock&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>knock</strong></a> it upwards and throw it to a teammate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">练习要求翻转球拍、抛球、把球向上击起，再把它投给队友。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not throw the ball hard: toss it slowly, flip your wrist and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=knock&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>knock</strong></a> it towards the target.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要用力投球：慢慢抛球，翻转手腕，把球击向目标。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">knock a ball away</span> · 把球击开</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aim&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">aim</a></span> · 瞄准</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=coordination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">coordination</a></span> · 协调</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The drill asks you to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flip&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flip</strong></a> the paddle, toss the ball, knock it upwards and throw it to a teammate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">练习要求翻转球拍、抛球、把球向上击起，再把它投给队友。</p></li>

### 词群 09.11｜滑行、跳跃与水上动作 {#group-09-11}

**串联方式：** 比较滑动、滑倒、滑翔和翻滚，再串起滑雪、滑冰、骑行、潜水和跳跃。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-138">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-138" class="vocabulary-word-title">slide<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-138" aria-label="定位 slide">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-138" data-mark-label="slide" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="slide：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="slide：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=slide&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 slide" title="在有道查询 slide">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-138</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.滑动；逐渐降低；n.滑行；下跌</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">You may glide smoothly at first, then slip, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=slide&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>slide</strong></a> downhill and tumble into the snow.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">你可能起初滑得很顺，随后失足、滑下坡并滚进雪里。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The instructor showed us how to glide without letting the ski slip sideways, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=slide&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>slide</strong></a> away or cause us to tumble.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练示范了如何平稳滑行，避免雪板侧滑、滑走或使我们摔倒。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Watch the skater glide across the rink, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=slide&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>slide</strong></a> to a stop and recover from a slip without a tumble.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">看那位滑冰者滑过冰场、滑停，并在打滑后恢复平衡而没有摔倒。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">slide downhill</span> · 向坡下滑</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=friction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">friction</a></span> · 摩擦力</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=balance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">balance</a></span> · 平衡</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-139">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-139" class="vocabulary-word-title">slip<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-139" aria-label="定位 slip">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-139" data-mark-label="slip" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="slip：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="slip：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=slip&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 slip" title="在有道查询 slip">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-139</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.滑跤；滑落；溜；n.滑倒</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">You may glide smoothly at first, then <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=slip&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>slip</strong></a>, slide downhill and tumble into the snow.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">你可能起初滑得很顺，随后失足、滑下坡并滚进雪里。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The instructor showed us how to glide without letting the ski <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=slip&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>slip</strong></a> sideways, slide away or cause us to tumble.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练示范了如何平稳滑行，避免雪板侧滑、滑走或使我们摔倒。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Watch the skater glide across the rink, slide to a stop and recover from a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=slip&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>slip</strong></a> without a tumble.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">看那位滑冰者滑过冰场、滑停，并在打滑后恢复平衡而没有摔倒。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">slip on ice</span> · 在冰上滑倒</p>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">You may glide smoothly at first, then slip, slide downhill and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tumble&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tumble</strong></a> into the snow.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">你可能起初滑得很顺，随后失足、滑下坡并滚进雪里。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The instructor showed us how to glide without letting the ski slip sideways, slide away or cause us to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tumble&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tumble</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练示范了如何平稳滑行，避免雪板侧滑、滑走或使我们摔倒。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Watch the skater glide across the rink, slide to a stop and recover from a slip without a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tumble&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tumble</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">看那位滑冰者滑过冰场、滑停，并在打滑后恢复平衡而没有摔倒。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">tumble down a slope</span> · 滚下斜坡</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=friction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">friction</a></span> · 摩擦力</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=balance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">balance</a></span> · 平衡</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-142">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-142" class="vocabulary-word-title">ski<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-142" aria-label="定位 ski">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-142" data-mark-label="ski" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="ski：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="ski：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ski&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 ski" title="在有道查询 ski">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-142</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.滑雪；n.滑雪板</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">You can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ski&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ski</strong></a> in winter, skate at the indoor rink and enjoy cycling in summer.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">你可以冬季滑雪，在室内冰场滑冰，夏季享受骑行。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The resort rents equipment for visitors who <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ski&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ski</strong></a> or skate and provides maps for cycling.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">度假村为滑雪或滑冰游客出租装备，也提供骑行地图。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She learned to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ski&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ski</strong></a> and skate as a child before taking up competitive cycling.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她小时候学会滑雪和滑冰，后来开始参加竞技骑行。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">ski downhill</span> · 滑雪下坡</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=helmet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">helmet</a></span> · 头盔</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=winter%20sports&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">winter sports</a></span> · 冬季运动</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-143">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-143" class="vocabulary-word-title">skate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-143" aria-label="定位 skate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-143" data-mark-label="skate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="skate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="skate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=skate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 skate" title="在有道查询 skate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-143</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.滑冰</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">You can ski in winter, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=skate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>skate</strong></a> at the indoor rink and enjoy cycling in summer.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">你可以冬季滑雪，在室内冰场滑冰，夏季享受骑行。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The resort rents equipment for visitors who ski or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=skate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>skate</strong></a> and provides maps for cycling.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">度假村为滑雪或滑冰游客出租装备，也提供骑行地图。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She learned to ski and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=skate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>skate</strong></a> as a child before taking up competitive cycling.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她小时候学会滑雪和滑冰，后来开始参加竞技骑行。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">skate on ice</span> · 滑冰</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=helmet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">helmet</a></span> · 头盔</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=winter%20sports&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">winter sports</a></span> · 冬季运动</li></ul>
</div>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-144">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-144" class="vocabulary-word-title">cycling<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-144" aria-label="定位 cycling">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-144" data-mark-label="cycling" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="cycling：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="cycling：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cycling&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 cycling" title="在有道查询 cycling">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-144</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.骑自行车运动</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">You can ski in winter, skate at the indoor rink and enjoy <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cycling&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cycling</strong></a> in summer.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">你可以冬季滑雪，在室内冰场滑冰，夏季享受骑行。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The resort rents equipment for visitors who ski or skate and provides maps for <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cycling&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cycling</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">度假村为滑雪或滑冰游客出租装备，也提供骑行地图。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She learned to ski and skate as a child before taking up competitive <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cycling&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cycling</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她小时候学会滑雪和滑冰，后来开始参加竞技骑行。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-145">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-145" class="vocabulary-word-title">dive<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-145" aria-label="定位 dive">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-145" data-mark-label="dive" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="dive：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="dive：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 dive" title="在有道查询 dive">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-145</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.跳水；潜水；俯冲</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dive</strong></a> or plunge into the river where branches drift past.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要在树枝漂过的河段跳水或纵身入水。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The swimmers watched a log drift away before deciding whether to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dive</strong></a> or plunge from the low platform.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">游泳者看着一段木头漂远后，才决定是否从矮台跳下或跃入水中。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A controlled <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dive</strong></a> is safer than a reckless plunge into water where strong currents may make you drift.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有控制的跳水比鲁莽跃入可能有强流让人漂走的水里更安全。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">dive into water</span> · 跳入水中</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=current&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">current</a></span> · 水流</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=buoyancy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">buoyancy</a></span> · 浮力</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-146">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-146" class="vocabulary-word-title">drift<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-146" aria-label="定位 drift">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-146" data-mark-label="drift" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="drift：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="drift：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drift&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 drift" title="在有道查询 drift">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-146</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.飘移，漂流；流浪，漂泊</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not dive or plunge into the river where branches <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drift&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>drift</strong></a> past.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要在树枝漂过的河段跳水或纵身入水。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The swimmers watched a log <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drift&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>drift</strong></a> away before deciding whether to dive or plunge from the low platform.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">游泳者看着一段木头漂远后，才决定是否从矮台跳下或跃入水中。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A controlled dive is safer than a reckless plunge into water where strong currents may make you <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drift&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>drift</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有控制的跳水比鲁莽跃入可能有强流让人漂走的水里更安全。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">drift downstream</span> · 向下游漂流</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=current&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">current</a></span> · 水流</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=buoyancy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">buoyancy</a></span> · 浮力</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-147">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-147" class="vocabulary-word-title">jump<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-147" aria-label="定位 jump">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-147" data-mark-label="jump" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="jump：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="jump：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=jump&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 jump" title="在有道查询 jump">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-147</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.跳；暴涨；跳远</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The children <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=jump&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>jump</strong></a> over ropes, hop on one foot, bounce balls and spring up for a final leap.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">孩子们跳过绳子、单脚跳、拍球，最后跃起做一次大跳。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The coach asked us to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=jump&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>jump</strong></a>, hop and bounce lightly before trying to spring forward in a long leap.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练让我们先轻跳、单脚跳和弹跳，再尝试向前跃起做一次大跳。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Watch a rabbit hop, spring over a root and land with a bounce after each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=jump&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>jump</strong></a> or leap.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">看兔子单脚式跳行、跃过树根，并在每次跳跃后弹动着落地。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">jump over a barrier</span> · 跳过障碍</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=agility&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">agility</a></span> · 敏捷性</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=landing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">landing</a></span> · 落地</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-148">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-148" class="vocabulary-word-title">leap<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-148" aria-label="定位 leap">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-148" data-mark-label="leap" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="leap：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="leap：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=leap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 leap" title="在有道查询 leap">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-148</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v，跳，跃；n.跳跃</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The children jump over ropes, hop on one foot, bounce balls and spring up for a final <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=leap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>leap</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">孩子们跳过绳子、单脚跳、拍球，最后跃起做一次大跳。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The coach asked us to jump, hop and bounce lightly before trying to spring forward in a long <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=leap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>leap</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练让我们先轻跳、单脚跳和弹跳，再尝试向前跃起做一次大跳。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Watch a rabbit hop, spring over a root and land with a bounce after each jump or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=leap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>leap</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">看兔子单脚式跳行、跃过树根，并在每次跳跃后弹动着落地。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">take a leap</span> · 纵身一跃</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=agility&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">agility</a></span> · 敏捷性</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=landing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">landing</a></span> · 落地</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-149">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-149" class="vocabulary-word-title">plunge<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-149" aria-label="定位 plunge">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-149" data-mark-label="plunge" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="plunge：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="plunge：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=plunge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 plunge" title="在有道查询 plunge">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-149</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.纵身投入；猛跌；(使)投身；n.跳水；猛跌</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not dive or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=plunge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>plunge</strong></a> into the river where branches drift past.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要在树枝漂过的河段跳水或纵身入水。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The swimmers watched a log drift away before deciding whether to dive or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=plunge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>plunge</strong></a> from the low platform.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">游泳者看着一段木头漂远后，才决定是否从矮台跳下或跃入水中。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A controlled dive is safer than a reckless <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=plunge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>plunge</strong></a> into water where strong currents may make you drift.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有控制的跳水比鲁莽跃入可能有强流让人漂走的水里更安全。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">take the plunge</span> · 纵身跳下</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=current&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">current</a></span> · 水流</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=buoyancy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">buoyancy</a></span> · 浮力</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The coach asked us to jump, hop and bounce lightly before trying to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spring&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spring</strong></a> forward in a long leap.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练让我们先轻跳、单脚跳和弹跳，再尝试向前跃起做一次大跳。</p></li>

### 词群 09.12｜安排一次户外活动 {#group-09-12}

**串联方式：** 从露营、野餐和狩猎进入竞赛、步行、远足、慢跑与旅行安排。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-153">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-153" class="vocabulary-word-title">tent<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-153" aria-label="定位 tent">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-153" data-mark-label="tent" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="tent：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="tent：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 tent" title="在有道查询 tent">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-153</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.帐篷</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At camp, we pitched a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tent</strong></a>, enjoyed a picnic and joined a treasure hunt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在营地，我们搭起帐篷、享用野餐并参加寻宝游戏。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The picnic beside our <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tent</strong></a> ended when the camp leader announced a treasure hunt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们帐篷旁的野餐在营地领队宣布寻宝游戏时结束了。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Families at the camp can rent a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tent</strong></a>, buy a picnic basket and follow clues in a treasure hunt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">营地家庭可以租帐篷、买野餐篮，并在寻宝游戏中追寻线索。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">pitch a tent</span> · 搭帐篷</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=campsite&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">campsite</a></span> · 露营地</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sleeping%20bag&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sleeping bag</a></span> · 睡袋</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-154">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-154" class="vocabulary-word-title">camp<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-154" aria-label="定位 camp">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-154" data-mark-label="camp" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="camp：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="camp：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=camp&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 camp" title="在有道查询 camp">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-154</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.营地；阵营</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=camp&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>camp</strong></a>, we pitched a tent, enjoyed a picnic and joined a treasure hunt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在营地，我们搭起帐篷、享用野餐并参加寻宝游戏。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The picnic beside our tent ended when the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=camp&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>camp</strong></a> leader announced a treasure hunt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们帐篷旁的野餐在营地领队宣布寻宝游戏时结束了。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Families at the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=camp&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>camp</strong></a> can rent a tent, buy a picnic basket and follow clues in a treasure hunt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">营地家庭可以租帐篷、买野餐篮，并在寻宝游戏中追寻线索。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-155">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-155" class="vocabulary-word-title">picnic<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-155" aria-label="定位 picnic">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-155" data-mark-label="picnic" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="picnic：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="picnic：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=picnic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 picnic" title="在有道查询 picnic">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-155</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.野餐；野餐食物；v.野餐</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At camp, we pitched a tent, enjoyed a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=picnic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>picnic</strong></a> and joined a treasure hunt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在营地，我们搭起帐篷、享用野餐并参加寻宝游戏。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=picnic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>picnic</strong></a> beside our tent ended when the camp leader announced a treasure hunt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们帐篷旁的野餐在营地领队宣布寻宝游戏时结束了。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Families at the camp can rent a tent, buy a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=picnic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>picnic</strong></a> basket and follow clues in a treasure hunt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">营地家庭可以租帐篷、买野餐篮，并在寻宝游戏中追寻线索。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">have a picnic</span> · 野餐</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=campsite&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">campsite</a></span> · 露营地</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sleeping%20bag&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sleeping bag</a></span> · 睡袋</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-156">

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At camp, we pitched a tent, enjoyed a picnic and joined a treasure <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hunt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hunt</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在营地，我们搭起帐篷、享用野餐并参加寻宝游戏。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The picnic beside our tent ended when the camp leader announced a treasure <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hunt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hunt</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们帐篷旁的野餐在营地领队宣布寻宝游戏时结束了。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Families at the camp can rent a tent, buy a picnic basket and follow clues in a treasure <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hunt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hunt</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">营地家庭可以租帐篷、买野餐篮，并在寻宝游戏中追寻线索。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-157">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-157" class="vocabulary-word-title">race<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-157" aria-label="定位 race">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-157" data-mark-label="race" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="race：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="race：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=race&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 race" title="在有道查询 race">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-157</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.赛跑，速度竞赛；人种，种族</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">During the marathon <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=race&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>race</strong></a>, a pedestrian watched each runner maintain a steady pace, step after step.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">马拉松比赛中，一位行人看着选手一步接一步保持稳定速度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A pedestrian must not step onto the marathon route while the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=race&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>race</strong></a> is moving at full pace.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">比赛全速进行时，行人不得踏入马拉松路线。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=race&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>race</strong></a> organiser kept the pedestrian crossing closed while marathon runners passed at a brisk pace, each step carefully measured.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">马拉松选手以轻快步速经过时，赛事组织者关闭了人行横道，选手每一步都谨慎控制。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">win a race</span> · 赢得比赛</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=endurance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">endurance</a></span> · 耐力</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=finish%20line&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">finish line</a></span> · 终点线</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">During the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=marathon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>marathon</strong></a> race, a pedestrian watched each runner maintain a steady pace, step after step.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">马拉松比赛中，一位行人看着选手一步接一步保持稳定速度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A pedestrian must not step onto the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=marathon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>marathon</strong></a> route while the race is moving at full pace.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">比赛全速进行时，行人不得踏入马拉松路线。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The race organiser kept the pedestrian crossing closed while <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=marathon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>marathon</strong></a> runners passed at a brisk pace, each step carefully measured.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">马拉松选手以轻快步速经过时，赛事组织者关闭了人行横道，选手每一步都谨慎控制。</p></li>
</ol>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">During the marathon race, a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pedestrian&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pedestrian</strong></a> watched each runner maintain a steady pace, step after step.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">马拉松比赛中，一位行人看着选手一步接一步保持稳定速度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pedestrian&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pedestrian</strong></a> must not step onto the marathon route while the race is moving at full pace.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">比赛全速进行时，行人不得踏入马拉松路线。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The race organiser kept the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pedestrian&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pedestrian</strong></a> crossing closed while marathon runners passed at a brisk pace, each step carefully measured.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">马拉松选手以轻快步速经过时，赛事组织者关闭了人行横道，选手每一步都谨慎控制。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Our vacation included a river cruise and a short <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=excursion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>excursion</strong></a> during the return trip.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们的假期包括河上游船，以及返程中的一次短途游。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The travel agent added a city <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=excursion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>excursion</strong></a> to the cruise trip for our family vacation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">旅行社为我们的家庭假期游轮行程增加了一次城市短途游。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For a relaxing vacation, book a cruise and take an optional <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=excursion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>excursion</strong></a> during the trip.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">想度过轻松假期，可以预订游轮，并在旅途中参加可选的短途游。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a day excursion</span> · 一日短途游</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=itinerary&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">itinerary</a></span> · 行程</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shore%20excursion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">shore excursion</a></span> · 岸上游览</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-166">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-166" class="vocabulary-word-title">hike<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-166" aria-label="定位 hike">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-166" data-mark-label="hike" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="hike：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="hike：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hike&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 hike" title="在有道查询 hike">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-166</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v./n.徒步旅行，远足</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We usually jog in the park, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hike&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hike</strong></a> at weekends and stride through the station on workdays.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们通常在公园慢跑，周末徒步，工作日大步穿过车站。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">You can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hike&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hike</strong></a> up the trail, jog down the gentle slope and stride across the meadow.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">你可以沿小径徒步上山，沿缓坡慢跑下山，再大步穿过草地。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than jog the whole route, we planned to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hike&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hike</strong></a> the hills and stride along the flat sections.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们计划在山路徒步、在平路大步走，而不是全程慢跑。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">hike a trail</span> · 沿小径徒步</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=walking%20boots&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">walking boots</a></span> · 徒步靴</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trail&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">trail</a></span> · 小径</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-167">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-167" class="vocabulary-word-title">jog<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-167" aria-label="定位 jog">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-167" data-mark-label="jog" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="jog：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="jog：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=jog&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 jog" title="在有道查询 jog">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-167</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.慢跑</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We usually <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=jog&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>jog</strong></a> in the park, hike at weekends and stride through the station on workdays.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们通常在公园慢跑，周末徒步，工作日大步穿过车站。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">You can hike up the trail, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=jog&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>jog</strong></a> down the gentle slope and stride across the meadow.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">你可以沿小径徒步上山，沿缓坡慢跑下山，再大步穿过草地。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=jog&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>jog</strong></a> the whole route, we planned to hike the hills and stride along the flat sections.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们计划在山路徒步、在平路大步走，而不是全程慢跑。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">jog in the park</span> · 在公园慢跑</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=walking%20boots&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">walking boots</a></span> · 徒步靴</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trail&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">trail</a></span> · 小径</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-168">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-168" class="vocabulary-word-title">stride<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-168" aria-label="定位 stride">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-168" data-mark-label="stride" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="stride：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="stride：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stride&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 stride" title="在有道查询 stride">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-168</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.大步走，阔步行走</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We usually jog in the park, hike at weekends and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stride&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stride</strong></a> through the station on workdays.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们通常在公园慢跑，周末徒步，工作日大步穿过车站。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">You can hike up the trail, jog down the gentle slope and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stride&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stride</strong></a> across the meadow.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">你可以沿小径徒步上山，沿缓坡慢跑下山，再大步穿过草地。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than jog the whole route, we planned to hike the hills and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stride&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stride</strong></a> along the flat sections.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们计划在山路徒步、在平路大步走，而不是全程慢跑。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">stride across a field</span> · 大步穿过田地</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=walking%20boots&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">walking boots</a></span> · 徒步靴</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trail&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">trail</a></span> · 小径</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 09.13｜描述行走的状态与用力 {#group-09-13}

**串联方式：** 漫步、逗留和落后之后，再用攀爬、拉拽与弯曲说明行动变化。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-169">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-169" class="vocabulary-word-title">wander<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-169" aria-label="定位 wander">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-169" data-mark-label="wander" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="wander：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="wander：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wander&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 wander" title="在有道查询 wander">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-169</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.闲逛，漫步；走柿，开小差</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wander&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wander</strong></a> off or linger at the viewpoint if you are already starting to lag behind.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果已经开始落后，就别走开闲逛或在观景点逗留。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Visitors who <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wander&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wander</strong></a> through the market and linger at every stall may lag behind the tour group.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在市场闲逛、每个摊位都逗留的游客可能会落在旅行团后面。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We let the children <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wander&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wander</strong></a> and linger by the stream, but made sure they did not lag too far behind.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们让孩子们在溪边闲逛逗留，但确保他们不会落后太远。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">wander through a village</span> · 在村里闲逛</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stroll&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">stroll</a></span> · 漫步</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=keep%20up&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">keep up</a></span> · 跟上</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="09-172">
<summary><h4 id="word-09-172" class="vocabulary-word-title">climb<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-09-172" aria-label="定位 climb">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="09-172" data-mark-label="climb" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="climb：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="climb：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=climb&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 climb" title="在有道查询 climb">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">09-172</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.攀登，爬</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Bend your knees, pull on the rope and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=climb&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>climb</strong></a> slowly rather than drag yourself up the rock.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">弯曲膝盖，拉住绳子慢慢攀爬，不要硬拖着身体上岩石。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We had to bend under branches, pull the cart and drag it over roots before we could <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=climb&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>climb</strong></a> the final hill.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们得弯腰穿过树枝、拉车并把它拖过树根，才能爬最后一座山。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=climb&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>climb</strong></a> with the equipment, bend carefully, pull the strap tight and avoid letting the bag drag behind you.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">带装备攀爬时，要小心弯腰、拉紧带子，并避免让包在身后拖地。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">climb a hill</span> · 爬山</p>

</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To climb with the equipment, bend carefully, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pull&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pull</strong></a> the strap tight and avoid letting the bag drag behind you.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">带装备攀爬时，要小心弯腰、拉紧带子，并避免让包在身后拖地。</p></li>

### 本章 Language Takeaway

优先尝试主动说出的三个表达：

- **attend a concert**：参加音乐会；attend 在第 16 章出现。
- **take regular exercise**：经常锻炼；regular 为补充词。
- **participate in a competition**：参加比赛；participate 在第 20 章出现。

### 串学示范｜自拟短文

People enjoy recreation in different ways. Some attend a concert or visit an exhibition, while others train at a gym or take part in a tournament. An athlete needs practice, and a musician needs rehearsal. Both activities may involve discipline, energy and support from other people.

**中文理解：** 人们享受休闲的方式各不相同。有些人参加音乐会或参观展览，另一些人在健身房训练或参加比赛。运动员需要练习，音乐家需要排练。两类活动都可能需要自律、精力和他人的支持。

### 闭卷回忆｜先尝试，再逐级提示

**任务：** 选择音乐或运动，说清参与者、场所、器具、准备和具体活动。

1. 关掉本章正文，在空白页列出你记得的词群；每群先写 3—5 个英文词或搭配。
2. 从中选 3 个词群，连成 3—5 句英文；用明确关系连接，专业分类和事实不确定时先核对。
3. 卡住时，先只看“本章主线”；还想不起，再看词群标题和中文简释，最后才看英文。
4. 用核对编号找出遗漏词；把“见到认得”与“想表达时能说出”分开记录。
5. 合上提示重新说一遍；下次换一个对象、地点或例子，检查能否迁移。

可直接复制的空白页：

```text
主题：娱乐运动
我记得的词群：
每群想起的词或搭配：
今天的 3 个 takeaway：
我的 3—5 句英文：
看提示后才想起的核对编号：
下次换什么情境再说：
```

**接到下一章：** 活动离不开器具和设备，进入第 10 章认识物品与材料。
